Markdown page “Custom REST source”. The Custom REST source lets you connect any REST API to
PostHog's Data Warehouse without waiting for a dedicated integration. You configure the
connection using the source builder in the PostHog UI – setting the base URL,
authentication, pagination, and the streams (endpoints) you want to sync – and PostHog
handles the rest.
